- **method-gauntlet**: Adversarial review in rotating roles. Each round wears one role (scrutinize, bug-hunter, security, a11y, editor, design-jury, edge-case) so it finds what the other roles structurally cannot, and each round FIRST re-verifies that the previous round's fixes actually landed before hunting new defects. Use to review a diff, a PR, a screen, a spec, or a milestone; when the user says review / loop 2 times / loop 3 times / เปลี่ยน role / adversarial pass / find what is wrong with this. - **method-greenlight**: The gate ABOVE the plan — build / buy / defer / don't, decided before any code or spec exists. Refuses to approve work whose success cannot be stated as an observable. Use before method-blueprint for anything of real size, or when the user asks ควรทำไหม / ทำเลยไหม / build this? / is X worth building / should we do this at all / do we even need it. - **method-groundwork**: Detects the project's stack and writes .method/config.yml — the file every other method-* skill reads for its commands, mediums, budgets, references, and model tiers. - **method-launch**: The pre-push and deploy discipline. - **method-of-moments-polygenic-regression**: Use when you need per-cell, pathway-level estimates of genetic association from GWAS summary statistics and a normalized single-cell Seurat object, e.g. to compute scPagwas.gPAS.score for trait-relevant cell identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/method-of-moments-polygenic-regression))
- **method-omics-qc**: Runs sequencing and omics QA: FASTQ, BAM/CRAM, VCF, count matrices, methylation arrays, sample swaps, batch confounding. Use when omics data needs QC before analysis. ([sajor2000/methodsmith](https://github.com/sajor2000/methodsmith/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodsmith/skills/method-omics-qc) · ★6)
- **method-optimize**: Runs bounded, metric-scored experiment loops to improve a model, prompt, or pipeline without touching the test set. Use when a scored metric must improve; broken runs go to method-debug. ([sajor2000/methodsmith](https://github.com/sajor2000/methodsmith/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodsmith/skills/method-optimize) · ★6)
- **method-orchestrator**: Orchestrator skill for the Method / System / Experimental Setup section - delegates writing to the method subagent (this file is the rulebook the subagent loads at startup), routes open questions to paper-researcher and reviews to paper-reviewer1/2; the main conversation never writes prose itself. Use this skill whenever the user is writing the Method, System, Experimental Setup, Apparatus, or Implementation section of a paper — the chapter that describes the technical system or platform built before running the user study. Triggers include "Method", "Experimental Setup", "System", "実装", "手法"… ([JavaLangRuntimeException/manji-standard-paper-writing](https://github.com/JavaLangRuntimeException/manji-standard-paper-writing/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/method-orchestrator))
- **method-parameterized-spectra-transformation**: Use when a 1H-NMR spectra DataFrame (columns = chemical shifts in ppm, rows = samples) shows baseline distortions from incomplete relaxation, pulse sequence imperfections, magnetic field inhomogeneities, solvent effects, phase errors, or macromolecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/method-parameterized-spectra-transformation))
- **method-peps**: Use when a PEPS, iPEPS, CTMRG, 2D tensor-network, environment dimension, or classical partition-function reproduction needs method-level route and tool selection. ([QuantumBFS/quantum.harness](https://github.com/QuantumBFS/quantum.harness/tree/HEAD/skills/method-peps) · ★67 · 2 copies)
- **method-performance-comparison-tabulation**: Use when a newly proposed computational method's identification performance must be quantified against established baselines under controlled perturbations like added noise or spurious-correlation confounders. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/method-performance-comparison-tabulation))
- **method-phenotype**: Validates a computable phenotype against chart review: 2x2 table, PPV, NPV, sensitivity, specificity, Wilson intervals. Use when checking a phenotype or code list. ([sajor2000/methodsmith](https://github.com/sajor2000/methodsmith/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodsmith/skills/method-phenotype) · ★6)
- **method-picker**: Help a SYSEN 5300 student choose a defensible method — which distribution, which control chart, which capability index, which test, regression vs. DOE vs. RSM — by interrogating the question and the data first, then naming the trade-offs. Invoked as /methodpick. Never runs the analysis, designs the study, or writes the code. ([timothyfraser/sysen](https://github.com/timothyfraser/sysen/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/method-picker) · ★13 · 2 copies)
- **method-plan**: Creates the durable plan for data-science work: an implementation plan or, for studies, a statistical analysis plan (SAP). Use when the user asks to plan work or write a SAP. ([sajor2000/methodsmith](https://github.com/sajor2000/methodsmith/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodsmith/skills/method-plan) · ★6)
- **method-pm-audit**: Playbook for auditing PM-health across 15 dimensions (Discovery, Strategy, Prioritization, Roadmap, Specs, Metrics, Launch, Measure, Comms, Decisions, Pricing, Competitive, Feedback, AI-PM, Governance) and producing a structured report. The brain behind pm-auditor. ([VandanaAjayDubey111/great-pm](https://github.com/VandanaAjayDubey111/great-pm/tree/HEAD/plugins/great-pm/skills/method-pm-audit) · ★3)
- **method-polisher**: Academic Method section polisher for research papers. Use this skill whenever the user wants to improve, restructure, evaluate, or rewrite the Method (or Methods, Methodology) section of a research paper. Triggers include: "polish my methods section", "is my methodology clear enough", "help me describe my procedure", "my methods section is too vague", or uploading a paper and asking to improve the methodology description. Also trigger when the user asks whether their method section has enough detail for replication. ([Chano363/MultiAgent](https://github.com/Chano363/MultiAgent/tree/HEAD/.codebuddy/skills/academic-writing-suite/method-polisher) · ★1)
- **method-polyopt**: Use when a noncommutative polynomial optimization reproduction needs method-level route and tool selection — certified lower bounds on ground-state energy via the moment-SOS / SOHS (NPA-style) hierarchy solved as a semidefinite program, Bell-inequality maximum quantum violation, or state-polynomial / tracial optimization. Triggers include polynomial optimization, SOS / SOHS relaxation, moment-SOS hierarchy, NPA hierarchy, certified energy lower bound, Bell inequality, semidefinite relaxation, NCTSSoS. ([QuantumBFS/quantum.harness](https://github.com/QuantumBFS/quantum.harness/tree/HEAD/skills/method-polyopt) · ★67 · 2 copies)
- **method-power**: Calculates sample size and power for clinical, survival, cluster, and prediction-model studies, with a script, sweep, and SAP text. Use when the user asks for power or sample size. ([sajor2000/methodsmith](https://github.com/sajor2000/methodsmith/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodsmith/skills/method-power) · ★6)
- **method-ppt**: 根据用户的论文组会和课程汇报偏好，制作、修改可编辑PPT， 并编写与最终页序对应的口播稿。适用于论文分享、课程汇报、技术方案讲解， 或用户要求“按之前的汇报风格”“按我的习惯做PPT”时。 重点是原文依据、方法叙事、公式各项的作用、算法步骤与局部批注修改； 内容、页数、版式和深度随本次任务调整。单独的概念提问不自动触发文件修改。 ([alk233/method-ppt](https://github.com/alk233/method-ppt/tree/HEAD/skills/method-ppt))
